- Thorn (F4/M4) — Spirit Bear HUD (kills toward the bear out of 25/30, spawn countdown, overkill count), Thorn / Spirit Bear / spirit mob / Spirit Bow highlights (through walls is **cheat build only**; the cheat build also has two switches under its red header, **All Mobs Through Walls** - every mob of the fight except bats, the Spirit Bear included - and **Bats Through Walls**, each drawing its targets through walls with no line-of-sight test in the Spirit Mob color, Thorn and the bear keeping theirs), and stun-spot waypoints (three built in). It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- Lights Device (F7/M7 P3 S2) — **cheat build only.** Its own tab in the Dungeons cheat block, out of In Boss since 2026-10-15, with two halves that each have their own switch and settings and work with Secret Aura and In Boss on or off (the card is on while either half is). **Lights Aura** flicks the six Lights levers for you when they are in range: Pre-Flick Before S2 (every unlit lever once, so the device is lit when S2 opens) and Finish When S2 Opens (after S1 is cleared, an unlit lever - the last one completes it - or, with all six already lit, exactly one lever so the device registers); Aura Range (2.1-4.5, to the lever's nearest point, checked again as each flick is sent), Aura CPS (1-20, each gap varying a little around the average), Aura Swing Hand and Chat Feedback; a lever behind you gets your body - never your camera - turned to it for one tick first. **Lights Triggerbot** flicks only the Lights lever your crosshair is on, by the same rules, after its Triggerbot Delay (0-500 ms, rounded up to whole ticks) and at least its Triggerbot Cooldown after its last flick, with its own Triggerbot Swing Hand; it never moves your camera. Every lever is flicked at most once a run between the two halves, nothing after the device is done or past S2. Secret Aura and Secret Triggerbot never click these six levers on an F7/M7 run, and each half has its own place in the mod's one-click-a-tick queue, ahead of Secret Aura. Your old In Boss Lights settings move here once: the Aura is on if In Boss was on with either Lights option on, with the range, CPS, swing and chat it used
- Dungeon Score Calculator — live estimated score and rank (S+/S/A...) from the tab list, with an optional Skill/Explore/Speed/Bonus breakdown, secrets still needed for S+, crypts and deaths (a switch each), colors for the labels, sub-labels, values and good/bad marks, the bonus kills (mimic, prince and the Murkbat bat: Hypixel's "A Bat has been slain. +1 Bonus Score" to a player with the Murkbat shard's Rekindle attribute; each player's bat is +1, up to 5 a run as the wiki and Odin count it, a teammate's known from their "Bat Killed!" party call), and optional 270/300 titles and party messages (party messages off by default)
- M7 Wither Dragons — Phase 5 helper: per-dragon spawn timers in-world and on a HUD, spawn boxes, health, a "which dragon is yours" title using the power/class split rules, and ice-spray / arrow counters
- M7 King Relics — relic spawn countdown, highlight of the cauldron matching the relic you're carrying, your placement time, and a party placement summary
- Chunk Cache — every chunk you've loaded stays readable in memory after the game would unload it, so the Interactive Map, its pathfinder, secret waypoints and the solvers still see rooms you've already visited; memory limit with oldest-first eviction, a Clear Cache button and a live cached-chunk readout. Per world, cleared on every world change; nothing extra is rendered, nothing in a cached chunk ticks, and collision/"is it loaded" stay exactly as they are without the feature
- Chocolate Factory Helper — legit, SkyHanni-style, and never clicks: inside the Chocolate Factory menu it outlines the best-value upgrade green and other affordable ones yellow, outlines stray rabbits gold with a ping and chat line when one appears, reminds you once per visit when the Time Tower has a charge and is not running, and shows chocolate per second, the best upgrade, its cost and the time until you can afford it above the menu.. Its info line above the menu is its own element in Edit HUD (Chocolate Factory Info Line), placed over the open factory menu
- Fairy Souls — missing-soul waypoints and a route to the souls you have not found (SkyHanni-style island graphs, downloaded at runtime), a per-profile found log that syncs from Hypixel's own Fairy Souls menu, and a HUD. The tab, top down: the switch (turning it off stops the guide - there is no Stop button), Show Waypoints, Show Path with its Nearest / Whole Island mode, Dim Path and Path Color, the Visible Path, Line Width, Re-Path After and Text Scale sliders, then HUD and Start On Island Join; Target Label, Target Color, Chat Feedback and the found-log buttons below. `/k560path` still navigates anywhere on the island and `/k560path souls ...` drives the guide. **Cheat build only: Auto Fairy Souls**, its own tab and one switch - it needs nothing else on, not even Fairy Souls (it loads the island graph and runs the route itself). Auto Walk and Auto Click are parts of it, both ON; with Auto Walk off you walk and it still clicks each soul you reach. Started with its Start/Stop key or `/k560path souls auto` (no menu button); while it runs your camera is free (it turns only your body, like Auto Routes), moving the mouse looks around, and any key or mouse button you press stops it, by name. Its keys come from a model of your real movement - speed attribute (Skyblock speed, Speed, sprint), friction, momentum, air control and Jump Boost - so it brakes for corners and stops at the soul at any speed instead of running past. Its turning draws a fresh human movement for every turn (varied speed, sometimes a small overshoot and correction, a little jitter, now and then a pause); there is no Turn Speed setting. It clicks each soul once it is in reach (eye to hitbox, 3 blocks), through the one-action-a-tick gate. Off the route - in water, lava, a pit, fallen off a ledge, knocked off, or stuck on a wall - it plans a way back over the loaded blocks (swimming up and out, ladders, jumps its Jump Boost clears) and carries on; where there is none it escapes by etherwarp in the Etherwarp modes (with an etherwarp item in the hotbar), else by Ender Pearl in Walk With Pearls mode (pearls in the hotbar), else says No way out once and skips that soul. Modes: Walk Only (never warps or throws a pearl), Walk With Pearls (walks; throws an Ender Pearl only to escape), Etherwarp (warps that save walking), Fast Etherwarp (warp chains). Sprint can be turned off. Failsafes stop it with the alarm on a world change (lobby, limbo) or disconnect. Damage does not stop it (Shellwise); knocked back, it re-plans from where it landed; if you die, it waits for the respawn (whose teleport is not taken for a correction or a staff check) and re-plans from there. A server correction or a server-set rotation sounds the alarm and it carries on.
- F7 Spots (F7/M7) — your own walk-to waypoints per phase (box, beam, label, distance), a Storm crush timer HUD with purple-pad highlight, and Last Breath aim-spot markers filtered to your class (plus NoammAddons' arrow-stack points and Devonian's Last Breath waypoints for the five P5 dragons). It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- P3 Nav (F7/M7) — highlights the Phase 3 section gate until it's actually destroyed, and boxes every unfinished terminal, lever and device in your section, each box vanishing the moment it's completed (through walls is **cheat build only**). It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- Rag Axe — Ragnarock Axe helper: cast/cancel alerts, separate channel (3s), buff (10s) and cooldown (20s) countdowns, the Strength gained message with an optional party announce, and built-in "rag now" prompts with per-prompt lead times for M7 P5 dragons (line + per-dragon spawn), Necron's drop-down, Storm, and F5/M5 Livid
- Blessings — tracks each dungeon blessing your run has (Power, Time, Wisdom, Stone, Life) from the tab list on a movable HUD, with optional Roman numerals. Levels only — no stat estimates
- Maxor's Crystals (F7/M7 P1) — Energy Crystal respawn countdown, how long you took to place yours (with a saved personal best, wiped only on a two-click confirm), a warning while you're still holding an unplaced crystal, an active-crystals counter, and an optional highlight of the crystals. It has no switch over the whole: each part has its own, they work independently of each other, and its menu card is ON while any of them is on and OFF only when all are (clicking a card that is OFF turns on its first part; clicking one that is ON turns every part off). A saved file from before 2026-10-15 with the old whole-feature switch OFF loads with every part OFF; with it ON, every part keeps its own setting
- Dungeon Run Summary — one saved record per finished run (floor, total time, splits, device/terminal times, secrets, crypts, deaths, failed puzzles, the mod's estimated score next to Hypixel's real Team Score, class and party size, the party with each player's class, rooms, secrets and deaths, which rooms each of them cleared and what killed them, and the dungeon map with every room's rotation), a history browser, per-floor personal bests (a run only counts, and is only announced, when it was actually finished with the boss defeated and Hypixel's Team Score reached the "PB Minimum Score" setting, S 270 by default or S+ 300; a wipe, a death-ended run, a warp out or a lower score never sets or announces one, and a stale PB mark on such a run is cleared when the game loads), and an optional compact chat summary (off by default so it doesn't repeat Split Timers)
- Posmsg — position-message waypoints for the F7/M7 boss fight: each one draws a ring on the ground and types its own message into party chat ("at hee2" — no coordinates, so teammates need no mod to read it) the moment you walk inside it. Completely inert outside the boss. Presets for the real spots (Simon Says/EE2/EE3/Outcore/Recore/Necron's Platform/P5) ship pre-positioned with real coordinates, plus custom ones you add; each has its own message, radius slider, ring toggle, color, label text scale and height, and an optional "only send once per run". Every waypoint starts off — turn on the ones you want
- Advanced Position — your exact X/Y/Z on a movable HUD with 0-8 decimal places (F3 shows 3), plus optional facing (raw running yaw with its wrapped value, and pitch), the block you stand on, and velocity in blocks per tick. While something is holding your camera (an align, an auto puzzle repositioning), the facing lines follow where you are actually LOOKING rather than the body angle the automation is holding you at, since that is the one your mouse is moving; the body yaw is still shown, dimmed, after it, because that is the one the server receives and the one an AP3 node is made from
- Run HUDs — two separately placeable HUDs: a Secrets HUD (secrets found this run plus the current room's count, in a text color you pick with the Secrets Text Color setting, white by default; shown during the clear only - it hides from the moment you enter the boss room on any floor until the next run, and the HUD editor still shows it so it can be moved) and the Score HUD from Score Calculator, which now also owns the 270/300 messages and mimic/prince/bat kill alerts (dungeons only). The run/no-lag timer used to be a third "Time HUD" here - removed, that belongs to Split Timers now
- Dungeon Alerts — Shadow Assassin alert, secret-collected sound, F6/M6 Terracotta timers, Spring Boots height HUD (with a height box in your color, drawn as an outline, filled, or both), dungeon class colors on nametags and tab, and room-entry alerts. The Dungeon Alerts tab (Shadow Assassin Alert and Room Alerts) has a switch for each alert and none over both: the alerts work independently, and the menu card is ON while either is on and OFF only when both are (an old file with the former both-at-once switch OFF loads with both alerts OFF). Room Cleared only alerts for the room you are standing in, and only when you saw it uncleared and then saw it clear (walking into a room a teammate already cleared is silent); the on-screen text says just "Room Cleared!" or "Secrets Done!" with no room name, nothing is sent to chat, and the On-screen Text switch is gone because the text is the alert. Cleared Color and Secrets Color set the two texts' colors, and the Shadow Assassin Alert has an Alert Color (purple by default)
- Party Interop — one place for what the whole party knows about the run. Works out what it can from what Hypixel already sends every client (the action bar's per-room secret count, terminal/device/lever lines, blood door and Watcher lines, the tab list's totals), reads the party-chat announcements other dungeon mods make (NoammAddons, Odin, Devonian, QUOI, Skytils markers) so you benefit without installing any of them, and leaves a seam for the mod's own relay. Every fact records where it came from and the most trustworthy source wins. Optional read-only bridge to NoammAddons/Odin if you happen to run them. On by default (turn it off to stop sharing and receiving); dungeons only
- Teammate Highlight — boxes your dungeon party in the world colored by their class, with optional name and distance labels, a self toggle and a dead-teammate skip; legit mode only shows a teammate you can actually see, the **cheat** build can box through walls
- Mob ESP — highlights star-tier dungeon mobs by name filter (real vanilla Glowing); legit mode only glows what you can already see via a real raycast, the **cheat** build can glow through walls too
- Mapping — a data-gathering tool for map research ("Dump Held Map Now"); Funny Map, the mimic room and the class-color heads it used to reserve are real settings of the Dungeon Map now
- Etherwarp Waypoints — mark secret / etherwarp spots per room: /ew waypoint add [name] boxes the block you're standing on (centered), saved against the room so it shows in that room on every run. /ew delete (or /ew remove) deletes the waypoint you stand on, /ew delete <n> waypoint #n of this room, /ew clear every waypoint in the room you're in, and /ew undo and /ew redo step back and forward through the last 50 adds, deletes and clears. One toggle turns the whole section on or off
- Custom Leap Menu — replaces the Spirit Leap GUI (the real chest and your inventory are hidden) with 4 big name boxes in your Leap Order, colored by class; click a quarter of the screen or press 1-4 to leap (scale 50-400%); Show Player Heads puts each teammate's face in their box; Leap Style Map in Clear shows the dungeon map during the clear with each teammate's face where they stand - click a face to leap - and the boxes again in boss; ready the moment Hypixel sends the menu, nothing shifts while a leap is in flight, and no head is drawn on the cursor after a click
- Item Protection — protect items (hover + Protect Key, by Skyblock UUID or optionally item id, or by a typed name) and dungeon-starred items, so a Hyperion can't be dropped, thrown out of a menu, sold, salvaged, traded, auctioned or anvil-fed by accident; every block says so in chat. Each protected item shows a tiny five-point star in its slot's top-right corner, which stays while you hover it and sits under the tooltip (starred items protected by Auto-Protect Starred get no marker). Lock In Place stops protected items being clicked at all, even to move them around your own inventory - this replaced Slot Lock, whose locked slots were carried over to their items' UUIDs. Prevent Hotbar Drops swallows the drop key while you hold a protected item; with Confirm To Force on, pressing drop 2-4 times (Drops To Force), each within 3 seconds of the last, drops it anyway
- Slot Binds — link two slots in your real inventory (one must be a hotbar slot); shift-click either one to swap it with its bound partner using the same real vanilla mechanic pressing a number key over a slot uses. To make a bind, hover a slot and press the bind key, then hover the partner slot and press the bind key again (pressing it over a bound slot deletes that bind). You can also hold the bind key over a slot and release it over a hotbar slot to swap the two; this does not make a bind. If Protect Item uses the same key, a quick tap over an item toggles its protection and a drag is only the swap
- Party Commands — Odin's party commands and "!" replies in one list: !warp, !wt, !allinvite, !ptme, !invite, !kick, !kickoffline, !reinv (kick the teammate who asked and invite them back 5 seconds later), !demote, !promote, !f1-!m7/!t1-!t5, !dt, !undt, !boop, !help, and the replies !coords, !ping, !fps, !time, !holding, !cf, !8ball, !dice, !tps, !location and !odin. Three sections: the main toggle (with a reply delay, 200 ms by default, so Hypixel does not answer "You are sending commands too fast"), which chats may use them (party, guild, all chat, Mod Chat, DMs, co-op; replies go back where they were asked), and one toggle per command. Anything that changes the party or the run only works for someone actually in your party or current dungeon run, whatever chat it came from; a DM'd !invite invites the sender. Only chat that really came from the server (or the relay) counts, it is rate limited per player and overall, and everything a party command does is printed in your own chat Every command starts ON once the feature is switched on (nothing is on until you do); of the chats, Guild (gc) and All Chat (ac) start OFF and the rest on.
- Door Keys — marks a real dropped Wither/Blood Key the moment it appears with its pickup range (Hypixel SkyBlock 0.27.2 added 5 blocks to the key pickup range, so the range is the Key Base Range setting, 1 block by default, plus 5). **Pickup Range** picks how: Radius, a flat circle at the key's own height; Sphere, a see-through shaded ball of that radius around the key - step inside it and the key is yours. **Circle Thickness** sets the circle's line width, **Circle Height** moves the Radius circle up or down from the middle of the key's stand (it is drawn there, not at the stand's feet), and **Circle Color**, **Tracer Color** and, on Sphere, **Sphere Fill** each take a color of their own (Auto follows the key: near-black for a Wither Key, red for a Blood Key; right click a color to put it back to Auto). An optional tracer line (adjustable thickness) and, in the cheat build, an ESP option that shows them through walls
- Wither Doors — highlights the Wither and Blood doors the dungeon map in your hotbar shows, and nothing it does not: a door appears once a room it leads from is opened on the map, and nothing is drawn before Mort's countdown ends and the run starts (in the sim, before /start's countdown says GO), since there is no map to read until then. Each door is a box with a waypoint label over it ("Wither Door 34m", readable through walls; **Door Labels**, on by default, turns the labels off). Wither doors are red while the team has no Wither Key and the nearest one turns green once it has one; the Blood door has its own **Blood Door Color** and **Blood Door (Key) Color** (it used to need Show All Doors). A wither door the world shows open is dropped, as the map does a moment later. Render Distance 32-256 blocks (96 by default). Off by default. **Style** draws the door as an Outline (the original look, and the default), a Fill, or a Filled Outline; **Fill Opacity** (5-100%, 35% by default) sets how strongly the fill shades the door. **Fill Color** is Same as Outline by default, so the fill takes the door's current color and a locked door still reads differently from one you have the key for; set it to Custom to fill every door with one **Custom Fill Color**. **Fairy Door** (both builds, on by default) highlights the door into the Fairy room in its own **Fairy Door Color** (pink, Fairy's map color) once you know it is one: the map draws that door like a wither door while its doorway is empty, so a map-wither door into an unopened room whose empty doorway you have actually looked at (a clear line of sight from your eyes) is the Fairy door. Until you have seen it, it is drawn as what the map says, a wither door. It clears when you or a teammate step into the room beyond, or the map shows it opened. The door on the far side of Fairy is not drawn while Fairy is unopened, and after that only once its block is loaded. In the cheat build, **Show All Doors** instead highlights every locked Wither door and the Blood door in the loaded world, before the map shows them and before the run starts, and draws the highlights - outline and fill alike - through walls; with it on, the Fairy Door is the door into Fairy on the path from the room you are in to Blood (the path Auto Blood Rush takes) from the start, taken from the map grid, and clears for the rest of the run as soon as you or any teammate steps into Fairy, the map shows Fairy opened, or the door itself opens
- Trajectories — predicts where a real bow shot or Ender Pearl throw would land using the real vanilla drag/gravity physics for each. A normal bow shows its path only while you draw it, at the speed the draw has reached. Shortbows (on by default): a Skyblock shortbow (lore "Shortbow: Instantly shoots!", or a TERMINATOR / *_SHORTBOW item id) fires instantly at full power, so its path shows the whole time you hold it, with one path per arrow: three for the Terminator, the side arrows 5 degrees to each side. Never fires anything
- Loadout Keybinds — on the real "(N/M) Loadout" screen, use number-row keys and left/right arrows to click a loadout slot or page instead of the mouse. The tab's **Open Loadouts** button runs /loadouts (sent straight to Hypixel) to open the Loadout menu, whether or not the keybinds are on; **Open Loadouts Key** is a bind (key or mouse button, off until set) that does the same from anywhere with no menu open. Each slot key has its own mode: **Only In Menu** (the default) clicks only while the Loadout menu is open; **Open, Swap, Close** works anywhere outside a menu (never while typing in chat): it runs /loadouts, waits for the Loadout menu, clicks that loadout one action per tick, then closes the menu it opened, and says so in chat if the menu never opens within 5 seconds
- Ability Keybinds — bind any key or mouse button to your real dungeon class Ability or Ultimate (the same real vanilla drop-stack/drop-item action Hypixel already reads), instead of the fixed Ctrl+Q/Q
- P4 Platform Highlight — highlights the real fixed 3x3 platform you need to mine after Goldor dies, before dropping into Necron's fight, and hides itself again once the platform has been mined out
- I Hate Diorite (cheat build only) — swaps Storm's real diorite pillars to see-through stained glass client-side during her fight
- Chat Keybinds — one tab, one switch (on by default), four sections. **Key Binds**: bind any key or mouse button (middle click included) to a command or chat message you type in yourself, each row with its own on/off; the old 8 menu binds are migrated automatically. **Catacombs / Master Mode** and **Kuudra**: /f0-/f7, /m1-/m7, /basic /hot /burning /fiery /infernal and /kuudra <tier> expand to Hypixel's own /joininstance command, one toggle per floor (F1 covers /f1 and /m1) and per Kuudra tier (a toggle applies the next time you join a world; hover one to see the exact command it sends). **Custom Shortcuts**: your own - a name you type (letters, digits and _, up to 32, without the slash) and the command it sends to the server, each with its own on/off, an optional key that sends the same command, and Delete. A name that is already a command (built-in, another mod's, Fabric's, vanilla's or the server's) is refused with the reason under the list, and custom shortcuts register as you edit them, no rejoin needed. Command Shortcuts used to be a tab of its own; its settings moved into Chat Keybinds on 2026-10-08 with nothing lost (a half that was switched off keeps its rows switched off)
- Revert Master Stars — shows Master Star items with the old all-red-stars look instead of Hypixel's numbered pip (cosmetic only)
- Inventory Search — Ctrl+F, or a click into the search bar at the bottom middle of the screen, to search by name/lore in any inventory-type screen; every matching slot is filled solid in the highlight color (red by default) behind the item. The bar is also a calculator like NEU's: type a sum such as 5k*3, 2s+10 or (1.5m-200k)/4 and it shows "= result" (+ - * x / ^, brackets, and the suffixes k, m, b, t, s = 64, e = 160 and %). Adjustable bar scale, Ctrl+Backspace word delete, and correct highlighting inside the Storage Overlay's own grid Enter in the bar: if it holds a sum, the sum is replaced by its answer (as plain digits) and typing stops.
- Pet Wheel — a radial keybind menu of the pets you choose; picking one summons it through /pets (hold-and-release or press-then-click; every pet you add gets its own slice and the wheel lays itself out; Scale grows the wheel and its text). The summon reacts to the menu instead of waiting on timers: /pets goes out the moment you pick, and the pet is clicked the first tick its page has loaded, so it takes about one round trip to Hypixel plus a tick or two per page. Walking, jumping and sprinting are held still until it finishes. Hide Pets Menu keeps the /pets menu from ever appearing while the wheel works - it still opens and closes behind the scenes. Hypixel has no command that summons a named pet directly, so the menu is the only route. The wheel's tiles and labels are drawn in the mod menu's theme (labels on small plates, so they stay readable over the world on Light); its Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can pick any preset or your Custom colors for the wheel alone
- Best Friends — /bestfriends: time partied and dungeon runs done with each player, kept forever, in a searchable, sortable menu. The list shows each player's time compactly ("1h 32m", "10d 3h 10m"); clicking a player shows it to the second ("1h 32m 5s"), counting up live while you are partied. Time is measured with the system clock between seeing a player join and leave the party (checked a quarter of a second at a time), kept to the millisecond, written every 30 seconds, on leaving the server and when the game closes. Typing anywhere on the list goes into its search box. Drawn in the mod menu's theme; its own Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or your Custom colors for this screen alone
- Friends List — our own friends list with notes; /fl can open it instead of Hypixel's, and /flcustom and /flhypixel always reach either one. A sync (Refresh, or opening the list when it is over ten minutes old) walks every page of Hypixel's /fl by following its own next-page arrow, about one page a second, and the /fl blocks it asks for are hidden from chat. A sync that loses its reply part-way keeps what it read and says the list may be incomplete. Each row shows the friend's real skin head (from the tab list when they are in your lobby, otherwise their profile is fetched once and cached), what the last sync said they were doing ("SkyBlock - Garden", "Offline", aged when it is old), and an Invite button that sends /party invite (at most one every two seconds; also on a friend's detail page). The scrollbar is wide, its thumb can be dragged, and clicking the track jumps there. While a sync runs, the line under the list shows how far it has got ("Syncing... page 3/7", from Hypixel's "Friends (Page 3 of 7)" header); that line has its own strip below the list and never covers a row. A search box under Add narrows the list by name as you type (any part of the name, upper or lower case); just typing on the list fills it, and clearing it shows everyone again. Drawn in the mod menu's theme; its own Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or your Custom colors for this screen alone
- Inventory Search — Ctrl+F, or a click into the search bar at the bottom middle of the screen, to search by name/lore in any inventory-type screen; every matching slot is filled solid in the highlight color (red by default) behind the item. The bar is also a calculator like NEU's: type a sum such as 5k*3, 2s+10 or (1.5m-200k)/4 and it shows "= result" (+ - * x / ^, brackets, and the suffixes k, m, b, t, s = 64, e = 160 and %). A number or a closing bracket next to a bracket multiplies, so 2(1+1), (1+1)(2) and (1+1)2 are 4; two plain numbers side by side (2 3) are still a search. Adjustable bar scale, Ctrl+Backspace word delete, and correct highlighting inside the Storage Overlay's own grid Enter in the bar: if it holds a sum, the sum is replaced by its answer (as plain digits) and typing stops.
- Pet Wheel — a radial keybind menu of the pets you choose; picking one summons it through /pets (hold-and-release or press-then-click; every pet you add gets its own slice and the wheel lays itself out; Scale grows the wheel and its text). The summon reacts to the menu instead of waiting on timers: /pets goes out the moment you pick, and the pet is clicked the first tick its page has loaded, so it takes about one round trip to Hypixel plus a tick or two per page. Walking, jumping and sprinting are held still until it finishes. Hide Pets Menu keeps the /pets menu from ever appearing while the wheel works - it still opens and closes behind the scenes. Hypixel has no command that summons a named pet directly, so the menu is the only route
- Best Friends — /bestfriends: time partied and dungeon runs done with each player, kept forever, in a searchable, sortable menu. The list shows each player's time compactly ("1h 32m", "10d 3h 10m"); clicking a player shows it to the second ("1h 32m 5s"), counting up live while you are partied. Time is measured with the system clock between seeing a player join and leave the party (checked a quarter of a second at a time), kept to the millisecond, written every 30 seconds, on leaving the server and when the game closes
- Friends List — our own friends list with notes; /fl can open it instead of Hypixel's, and /flcustom and /flhypixel always reach either one. A sync (Refresh, or opening the list when it is over ten minutes old) walks every page of Hypixel's /fl by following its own next-page arrow, about one page a second, and the /fl blocks it asks for are hidden from chat. A sync that loses its reply part-way keeps what it read and says the list may be incomplete. Each row shows the friend's real skin head (from the tab list when they are in your lobby, otherwise their profile is fetched once and cached), what the last sync said they were doing ("SkyBlock - Garden", "Offline", aged when it is old), and an Invite button that sends /party invite (at most one every two seconds; also on a friend's detail page). The scrollbar is wide, its thumb can be dragged, and clicking the track jumps there. While a sync runs, the line under the list shows how far it has got ("Syncing... page 3/7", from Hypixel's "Friends (Page 3 of 7)" header); that line has its own strip below the list and never covers a row
- Auction House Browser — one switch for the browser and for Hypixel's own Auction House drawn in its look (below). /killer560 ah, a keybind, or /ah with the /ah override on: every live auction from Hypixel's public API (scanned in the background every five minutes, page by page off the render thread, only new auctions decoded on a rescan, skipped entirely while Hypixel's snapshot has not changed) in one screen of the mod's own design. Hypixel's six categories (Weapons, Armor, Accessories, Consumables, Blocks, Tools & Misc) are tabs along the top, with a search field (type anywhere to search; names, item ids and catalog names all match) and Sort, Rarity, BIN only / Auctions only and Price range filters beside it; recent searches and recently viewed items sit on the left. Listings are cards with the item's real icon, its name in Hypixel's colors, the price (BIN, top bid or starting bid), a BIN or bid-count badge, time left and pet level, and the cheapest BIN of an item is outlined and marked Lowest BIN; hovering shows the item's full lore plus the lowest BIN, how many are listed and recent sale prices. Clicking a card sends Hypixel's own /viewauction for it and its real auction page opens in the same frame (see Reskin Real Auction House). Create Auction, Manage Auctions and View Bids are one button away through Hypixel's own /ah menu. Lowest BIN per item, an hourly lowest-BIN history (24 points, drawn as a sparkline) and recent sales from Hypixel's ended-auctions feed (polled once a minute only while the AH is in use) are kept across restarts. The screen follows the mod menu's theme (or its own Theme setting; Inventory Theme there gives the older look, the Inventory Theme's Amber, Dark or Light and its accent), and its backdrop is opaque so no HUD shows through. /hypixelah always reaches Hypixel's own menu
  - *Hypixel's own Auction House, reskinned* (the same switch: turning the Auction House Browser on or off turns this on or off too, since 2026-10-11) — Hypixel's real Auction House menus (the Auction Master, /ah, or a listing opened from the browser) open as pages of the same screen instead of the chest: the Auction House menu as cards, the Auctions Browser with its own category tabs, search sign, sort, rarity and BIN filters and page arrows in the header and toolbar and its listings as cards (price, BIN or bids, seller, time left, lowest-BIN marker), an auction's page with the item large, its price, seller and time left, a market panel (lowest BIN, listings, recent sales, 24-hour sparkline) and Hypixel's own Buy Item Right Now / Submit Bid / Bid Amount / Bid History buttons as cards, Confirm Purchase and Confirm Bid as a green Confirm and a red Cancel, Manage Auctions and Your Bids as listing cards with Sold and Expired badges, and Create Auction with your inventory drawn so you can pick the item. It is still Hypixel's real menu: each click you make is sent as the same single click on the same slot, only when you click and only if that slot still holds what was drawn; bid and price signs are Hypixel's own. Pages change without a flash: the previous page stays drawn until the next one has loaded. Menus it does not recognize (Auction Stats, Bid History) stay Hypixel's own; hold the Hypixel Menu key (Left Alt by default) or press Hypixel menu in the header to see Hypixel's. On by default; part of the Auction House Browser toggle
- Bazaar Browser — one switch, one Bazaar screen for browsing and for Hypixel's own Bazaar. Categories (Farming, Mining, Combat, Woods & Fishes, Oddities) are tabs across the top beside a search box; down the left is Recent, the products you opened or searched for most recently on this SkyBlock profile, newest first, one click from opening again; under it is your inventory, so you can list what you carry: with Hypixel's Bazaar open a click on an item there is the same click on that item in Hypixel's menu (which opens its product page to sell it or make a sell offer), and with no menu open it opens that product like a click in the list; anything the Bazaar does not sell is dimmed and does nothing; along the bottom are only Sell Inventory Now, Sell Sacks Now and Manage Orders. Browsing is drawn from the live Bazaar data and needs no menu open: a category lists its products under Hypixel's own groups (Wheat & Seeds, Gemstones...), each with its real icon, instant buy and sell price, margin and seven-day volume, and the Buy, Sell, Margin and 7d vol headings sort by that column; typing searches every category at once. Clicking a product opens it on Hypixel. With a Booster Cookie the screen sends Hypixel's own /bz with the product's name and shows the product (prices, the top buy orders and sell offers, and its four trade buttons filling in) until Hypixel's page arrives in the same place; with Follow-up Click on (the default) and exactly one search result being that product, it clicks that result once for you as the follow-up to your click, so you land on the product page - with none or several matches, or Follow-up Click off, the results show with the product highlighted and you click it. At the Bazaar NPC without a Cookie, each click makes the one menu click that leads toward the product (its category, then its group, then the product). /killer560bz or the keybind open the screen with no menu behind it, and like Hypixel's remote Bazaar only while your Booster Cookie is active (read from the tab list's Cookie Buff); without one, chat says so. /bz is always Hypixel's own command. The bottom buttons press Hypixel's real button when it is on the open page, and otherwise (remote, with a Cookie) send /bz and press it as the follow-up to your click. Hide HUDs in Bazaar (on by default) hides every HUD - bars, scoreboard, map, timers, overlays - while the Bazaar is open. The colors follow the mod menu's theme (or the Bazaar's own Screen Theme; Inventory Theme there gives the older look, the Inventory Theme's Amber, Dark or Light). Track My Orders reads your orders whenever Manage Orders is open (read only).
  - *Hypixel's own Bazaar, reskinned* (the same switch: turning the Bazaar Browser on or off turns this on or off too, since 2026-10-11) — Hypixel's real Bazaar (the Bazaar NPC, or /bz) opens as the Bazaar screen above instead of the chest: the main page is the browsing view, group pages and search results are product rows with buy and sell prices (and margin and weekly volume from the live data), the product page is the product with live prices over big Buy Instantly, Sell Instantly, Create Buy Order and Create Sell Offer cards, the amount, price and confirm steps of instant buys, buy orders and sell offers are cards showing Hypixel's own text, Manage Orders is your live order list (filled, price, total, claimable, and whether each is still the top order), and Go Back is the Back button in the header. Hovering anything shows the real item's full tooltip. It is still Hypixel's real menu: each click you make is sent as the same click on the same slot, only when you click and only if that slot still holds what was drawn, so buying, selling, orders, claiming, canceling and the custom amount signs all work exactly as before. Changing page does not flash: until Hypixel's next page has arrived, the page you were on stays exactly as it was (and cannot be clicked), and nothing in the header, tabs, recents or bottom bar moves. Screens it does not recognize (Bazaar Settings, History, Confirm Instant Buy) stay Hypixel's own. Hold the Hypixel Menu key (Left Alt by default) or press Hypixel menu in the header to see Hypixel's own menu. On by default.
- Listing Helper — on Hypixel's Create BIN Auction menu, shows the lowest BIN among similar listings with a similarity percentage and copies lowest-minus-one-coin to your clipboard. Never clicks or types into Hypixel's menu
- Party Dungeon Data — teammates running this mod share room secrets, rooms and doors found, mimic/prince/bat kills and M7 dragon spawns over the mod's own relay, feeding the map, score calculator and HUDs. The format is an open spec (relay repo PROTOCOL.md)
- Cross-Mod Bridge — connects to Devonian's, NoammAddons' and Odin's party sockets so teammates running those mods share data with you too, and your finds reach them. Skips any mod you already have installed, only ever sends your own name, logs in through Mojang's normal handshake. On by default, like every sharing setting; each mod can be switched off
- Team Melody HUD — live Melody terminal progress for every teammate in P3, from teammates on this mod, Odin users (via the bridge) and your own read-only tracker, which also shares your progress (Share My Progress stays on the tab with the HUD off; the Scale slider shows only with the HUD on)
- Teammate rooms on the map — rooms and doors your teammates found show on the dungeon map before you reach them, dimmed until you see them yourself (display only, never used by pathfinding)
- Inventory Theme — re-skins chest and inventory menus, and your hotbar, as vanilla's own GUI recolored: the bevelled panel (light top-left, dark bottom-right edges), each slot's square (vanilla's sunken bevel in Vanilla; in every other theme a one-unit outline in the theme's light shade on all four sides, so every slot is outlined top and left too), the player-model window, the crafting arrow and the hotbar, all drawn by the mod. The drawer has no Inventory Theme row (the card's switch is that setting). Its first row is **Theme**: Default (the theme the mod menu is on, and what new installs start with), then Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean and Viridian, any theme you saved, Vanilla (vanilla's own light gray, with its darker hotbar and selection ring) and Custom. On Custom a **Customize** row opens a menu with the Custom look's Base (Amber, Dark or Light), Slot Color Source / Slot Color and Accent Source / Accent Color; those overrides apply to Custom only. A file from before this carries its old preset over (Default became Vanilla, Dark became the menu's Dark, Mod Theme became Amber). Items, Item Rarity Backgrounds, the hover highlight, Inventory Search and Item Protection stars sit exactly on the slots, as in vanilla; the hotbar keeps its bottom edge on windows whose height is not a multiple of the GUI scale. Everything it draws is opaque. The selected hotbar slot gets vanilla's own ring in the theme's accent (a muted gray on Dark). Also: Applies To (Hypixel/p3sim menus only, or every container), Theme Hotbar with the Hotbar Scale slider, and the potion effects panel beside the inventory is always hidden while the theme is on (the Hide Potion Effects setting is gone).
- Mod conflict warnings — one chat warning per session if Skyblocker's terminal solver or Devonian's Party Finder Overview is doing the same job as ours
- Item Browser — a NEU-style full-height panel listing the complete Skyblock item catalog with real icons beside any inventory screen (only on inventory screens by default; the Only In Inventory toggle turns that off); adjustable column count (3-20), scale, horizontal/vertical fill order and left/center/right anchoring, hovering an item shows its real tooltip (lore from the NEU item repo, with its value), and a left-click craft and obtain popup with recipes from the NEU item repo (crafting grid with counts, forge, NPC shop and other ways to get it). Every minion is one stacked cell; clicking it opens a popup of all its tiers, and clicking a tier shows that tier's recipe. Sorts by value high to low by default (also low to high, A-Z, Z-A). It has no search box of its own: it filters by the Inventory Search bar at the bottom middle of the screen, so keep Inventory Search on to use them together
- Item Rarity Backgrounds — colors the slot behind every Skyblock item by its rarity (Common through Divine/Special/Ultimate, pets included) in inventories and optionally the hotbar, with Square/Circle/Outline styles (adjustable outline width) and adjustable opacity (visual only)
- Slot Text — small text in the corner of items in menus and your inventory, read from the item and the open menu: pet level (top left), enchanted book level (bottom left) and an optional short enchantment tag (top left, ultimates in purple), minion tier (top right), potion level and Attribute Shard level (bottom right), Rancher's Boots speed cap (bottom left), skill levels in Your Skills (bottom left, gold when maxed), collection tiers (bottom right), Bestiary and Fishing levels (bottom right, gold at 100%), Heart of the Mountain and Heart of the Forest perk levels, and the points in Stats Tuning. Each kind has its own switch. Show is Always, Hold Key to Show or Hold Key to Hide (default key Left Alt, a mouse button works too); Skyblock Only is on by default. Container slots only, not the hotbar; visual only, off until switched on
- Compactor Preview — hover a Personal Compactor or Personal Deletor (4000-7000) and its tooltip gets a grid of the items you set up in it (read from the item's own saved slots; 1, 3, 7 or 2x6 slots by tier, an empty one just says how many slots it has, a deletor also shows Active YES/NO), so you do not have to open it. Show is On Hover or While Holding Key (default Left Alt; a mouse button works too). Item icons come from the Item Browser's catalog when it is on, otherwise a look-alike vanilla item (enchanted ones glint). Visual only, off until switched on
- Storage Item Search — search every cached ender chest page, backpack, wardrobe page, Equipment Wardrobe page (/equipment), pet page, island chest and your inventory by name, id or lore; enchanted books are listed by their enchant. Click a result to open that storage with the item outlined, or - for island chests - to box every chest holding that item. A Scan All button opens every storage, wardrobe and pet page once so all of them are remembered; it reads Hypixel's Storage menu first and never opens a locked Ender Chest page or an empty backpack slot
- Waypoint Routes — per-area waypoint routes that advance as you reach each point, with keybinds to add/remove/skip, and clipboard import/export in ColeWeight and Skytils formats (an import keeps at most 64 routes of 2000 points each, with names and labels cut to 48 characters, and says so in chat when it cuts)
- Custom Mage Beam — replaces the mage beam particles with a clean colored beam (color, real thickness up to a full block, duration, fade, and a Shape: Square, the default, a square beam coming out; Flat, a horizontal ribbon; or Cylinder - the beam is fixed in the world, so it does not turn as you run round it)
- Auto Quiz / Auto Three Weirdos — **cheat build only.** Clicks the solver's answer in Quiz and opens the right chest in Three Weirdos. Walking into the Quiz room is what starts it on Hypixel (no click on Oruo); Auto Quiz presses the answer's button on the first tick Oruo's cooldown allows - 220 server ticks after his first line, 100 after "2 questions left..." and "One more question!" (NoammAddons' and Odin's timings, matched by his own Hypixel logs); an earlier press does not count. With Talk To NPCs on, Auto Three Weirdos talks to each weirdo itself, one a tick, within the 3-block entity reach; when the ones left are out of reach it etherwarps (Etherwarp Reposition, an AOTV in the hotbar) or walks (auto-puzzle pathing, the Interactive Map when no walk or warp line gets there) onto the spot that has as many of them, and their chests, in reach as it can, and does the same for the chest if it ends up out of reach. Chest Open Delay defaults to 0 (the chest is clicked the tick it is known and in reach); a config still at the old 250 ms default is moved to 0 once
- Auto Puzzles (QUOI port) — **cheat build only.** An auto for every other puzzle, each needing its solver on: Auto Blaze, Auto Creeper Beams and Auto Ice Path shoot your shortbow (shared Shoot cooldown), Auto Boulder starts when you walk into the room or any teleport (an Interactive Map trip or your own etherwarp) lands you on its doorway spot, and it sprints on every walk: with Secret Aura on and its Chests option on it runs straight along the barrier roof to the iron bars at the back and lets the aura take the chest, pressing nothing; otherwise it walks down the stairs (from the map's doorway spot it first steps inside the room, because the Boulder Solver only reads the boxes from inside), presses only the buttons the Boulder Solver names and opens the chest by looking at it, turning like a mouse would and drawn smoothly every frame, and either way it then gets you back out of the room so you can etherwarp again: once nothing but open floor at your height lies between you and the doorway (along the roof from the bars, or from the top of the stairs) it aims at a point just past the doorway and sprints straight there, so it runs in and out without stopping; its other walks slow for corners and stairs by letting go of forward in time, so they keep their line at any speed (your sim's speed 600 included), and it walks on to the next button as soon as the box the last press pushed has moved. Auto Water Board / Auto Tic Tac Toe click the right levers and cells (Auto Tic Tac Toe first etherwarps onto the block right in front of the board from which every square is in reach, and with its **Grab Secret** option on it etherwarps right after the first click onto a block within Secret Aura's reach of the room's secret chest (walls do not matter - the aura clicks through them), lets Secret Aura take it - or opens it itself if the aura will not - and etherwarps back in front of the board), Auto Teleport Maze walks to the right pad - the exit pad once the solver has narrowed it down, by the shortest way over the pads it has already learnt; otherwise it tries a new pad, and when its chamber has none left it goes back over known pads to the nearest chamber that does, so it never wanders back through the maze (round the walls in the middle of a chamber's sides rather than into them, and onto the start pad by itself when the Interactive Map has just brought you to the room), Auto Ice Fill steps the path with your AOTV (on each section's first tile it always waits for the server to register the tile before hopping on, adaptive or not; Adaptive mode waits like that on every tile, so lag can't make it fail), and if a section breaks under you it stops everything, waits until that section has come back as fresh ice (Hypixel brings back only the broken one, about two seconds later; it gives up with a chat message after 15 s), then etherwarps you back onto that section's first tile and carries on, leaving finished sections alone, plus an optional Etherwarp Reposition that warps you to each puzzle's standing spots. Auto Water Board clicks each timed lever on the tick the Water Board Solver's countdown reaches it: as soon as you stand where the next lever is in reach your body is already turned onto it, and the tick a lever is clicked your body turns to whatever comes next (the next lever, or the etherwarp toward it), so the warp goes the very next tick; when two clicks come too close together for a warp in between, it stands where both are in reach. Every other auto that warps or clicks works the same way (since 2026-10-15, no fixed waits): an etherwarp's use goes the tick after its sneak goes down (or in the same tick when the sneak is already held, as between the bow puzzles' warps), and the tick after a landing comes the next shot, click or warp - no 2-tick sneak wait, no 2-tick wait after the landing and no 6-tick pause after the teleport; a click on a block (a reward chest, Higher/Lower's secret chest, a Tic Tac Toe square after a warp) has the body already turned onto it in the landing tick, so it needs no turn tick of its own, and an auto's own free camera no longer makes its click wait for "somebody else" turning you. Auto Tic Tac Toe clicks a new move the tick after it reads it (only a retry of the same square still waits 500 ms) and starts the Grab Secret warp in the tick of its first click, and the warp back the tick the chest opens. Once Water Board, Ice Path or Ice Fill is solved, the auto opens the puzzle's reward chest itself (Secret Aura skips Ice Path and Ice Fill and holds off in Water Board), warping into reach of it first if it has to. A chest counts as opened the moment the server sends its window, even when Auto Close Chest hides it, so each chest is clicked once and the step ends that tick; a click with no answer is tried again as soon as the answer is overdue (your measured ping plus any server lag), and once a chest has been dealt with the auto never waits for another to appear. Autopilot, Auto Secret and Auto Routes carry on the tick that last step is over (Boulder's walk out, Higher/Lower's last kill and secret, Creeper Beams' fourth pair, the Three Weirdos chest, Quiz's final answer, the end of a Tic Tac Toe game, a Teleport Maze walk out, a reward chest), and also the tick Hypixel's "PUZZLE SOLVED!" line for you arrives rather than waiting for the map or tab list to show it, and while they wait on an auto puzzle each logs so once per room. From an auto's first aim or warp to its last (Blaze, Creeper Beams, Ice Path, Ice Fill, Water Board, Teleport Maze), your camera is in AP3's free camera: your body turns to every shot and warp exactly as the server sees it, your screen keeps your view (Higher/Lower starts it facing the room's center) and your mouse moves only the view; when the auto finishes or you leave the room, your body turns back to where you are looking and the camera is handed back with nothing moving. The shooting autos find their own shot: Auto Ice Path warps straight onto the silverfish's cell when it can see it, otherwise to a board cell that can, otherwise walks there with the Interactive Map; Auto Blaze judges spots from your real eye height, searches the room's ledges when none of the usual spots has a line, never sends a second volley before the first one's result is in, and moves on the moment it is (the target's kill reported, or none of the volley's arrows able to reach it any more - hit, stuck, or flying away; since 2026-10-15 there is no fixed margin on top), dropping a blaze whose kill is already reported from the order at once, and gives a spot up after three shots that kill nothing (a volley has no spread, so another from the same block flies the same way); with a Terminator it also takes a shot whose side arrow (5 degrees either side) is the one that reaches the blaze, when the center arrow has no clean line, picks the standing spot that can shoot the most blazes in a row, and when the next blaze has no shot from where you stand it warps to its spot while the volley at the current one is still in the air; the Shoot cooldown is counted in whole ticks (500 ms is exactly 10), and the room is done the tick the last blaze dies to its volley rather than 30 ticks later; with its **Auto Secret** option on it sets off for the room's secret the tick after the volley at the last blaze leaves the bow, without waiting for the kill, walks to the block within aura reach of the chest that is nearest the room's door rather than onto the chest, and takes it once that volley has landed (it waits at a chest that only appears when the room is done); if that last blaze survives, it still takes the secret first and then goes back and shoots until the room is done; Auto Creeper Beams aims at each lantern's center from where you actually stand, and with no clear line past the creeper it shoots anyway and logs so (on Hypixel that arrow can hit the creeper). Its **Creeper Beams Mode** setting (Shoot by default) can be set to Melee: instead of the bow it etherwarps to a spot within reach of each lantern (4.5 blocks to the lantern's box, with a clear look at it) and left-clicks it. A lantern no such spot reaches (one hung high up) is reached with a plain Aspect of the Void teleport that lands him beside it, and hit in the air the tick after; one neither reaches is shot with the shortbow, from where he stands or from a spot with a clear line that misses the creeper. It starts the cheapest pair it can finish (spots first, then teleports, shots last); a hit with no answer from the room (the hurt sound or the lantern changing) is tried again another way, and it never stops while a lit pair can still be finished. **Auto Puzzle Pathing** is one switch over every piece of walking the auto puzzles do - Water Board's start-area warp, Boulder's trip above the chest, Tic Tac Toe's room spot and chest trip, Higher/Lower's walk to the secret, and the walk out of a finished room. On by default, and with it off the solvers and their clicking still work but nothing moves you. Auto Tic Tac Toe also gained **Walk Out When Done**, off by default: when the game is over (a line of three or a full board, or the puzzle solved or failed on the tab list - not merely while the computer is thinking about its reply) it asks the live map for the nearest door to the room you are in and paths there, which works in any room rather than needing a hand-measured exit per room, and stands two blocks back from a locked door. It is off by default because it is the one auto-puzzle walk whose destination nobody has stood on and checked. Auto Quiz presses one of the correct pillar's **buttons** rather than the pillar itself, which is a plain smooth-stone block a right-click does nothing to. Every auto puzzle's wait for the server (an etherwarp reposition landing, a pad's teleport in the Teleport Maze, a Boulder button or box, Ice Fill's regenerating section, the Blaze chest) is extended by any server lag, so a lag spike does not end a solve; with no lag the timing is unchanged.
- Auto Dialogue / Breaker Aura — **cheat build only.** Picks NPC dialogue options (never purchases or trades; dungeons only unless "Outside Dungeons" is on), and breaks blocks you have PICKED with the Dungeonbreaker - **in a boss fight only** (the F7/M7 boss phase or any floor's boss room); during clear it breaks nothing, and the only automatic breaking is an Auto Routes breaker node. **Pick Block Key** (`;` by default, rebindable in the Breaker Aura tab): look at a block and press it to mark it; press it again on a marked block to unmark it. Marked blocks are drawn in orange, brighter once they are in reach, and they survive a restart. **Display** chooses how: Highlight (the default) draws them like any block outline, hidden behind walls; Waypoint draws every picked block through walls whenever it is within your render distance, however far out of reach. **Box Style** makes them Outline (the default), Fill, or Filled Outline, in either display. Broken picks are not drawn. Nothing else is ever broken: there is no automatic path mode any more (the old **Only Picked Blocks** switch and its **Side Reach** slider were removed 2026-10-05). A picked block breaks as soon as it is within your **Reach** - ahead of you, to your side, or the block you are standing on. The block you are standing on always disappears on your screen the moment its break is sent, Zero Ping or not, exactly as a block you break instantly by hand does, so you start falling on the same tick (with it kept until the server answered, GrimAC flagged the gap as standing on air). Picking works with the aura switched off, so you can mark a wall first and turn it on after, and it is disabled in Auto Routes edit mode so placing nodes cannot mark or break anything. **Multi Break** is the only rate control and it is a plain on/off. Off, one block goes out a tick - the rate a hand produces. On, every picked block in reach goes out on the same tick, limited only by the charges you actually have; the blocks in front of you are sent first. There is deliberately no number to tune: the goal is that you can never run into a wall while it is breaking, and how many blocks that takes depends on how fast you are moving, so any fixed cap is either doing nothing or too low. Measured 2026-09-27 against a live anticheat on a dedicated server, with the old path mode sprinting into a 930-block wall it one-shots: with Multi Break on and a corridor 1.6 wider either side it sent up to 10 breaks on a tick, cleared the entire wall, and crossed it at 0.54 blocks a tick - exactly the speed the same player managed on open ground with no wall at all - reporting a collision on 0 of 210 movement packets. The same run on the shipped defaults at the same speed collided on 17 of 219, and the anticheat reported 17 movement violations: the same 17. So the flags were the collisions. That is worth stating plainly in both directions - the slow setting is what desyncs you at speed, because you walk into blocks being removed on that same tick; and ten interaction packets in one tick is not a rate a hand can produce and is visible as exactly that. Both are off by default. A picked block counts as air to the AP3 route planner, so a route may be planned straight through a wall you intend to break
- Custom Party Finder — our own party finder, shared by every mod user through the mod's relay (/cpf, or Open Party Finder in its card). It opens on a start screen where you pick Dungeons, Diana, Fishing or Misc (any party that fits none of the others, with a typed activity); inside one, only its listings show, with a Back button to the start screen. Each listing shows the leader, the activity, the party size, its requirements and a note. A search box matches the leader's name, the activity and the note; filters show one floor only, only parties whose requirements you meet, and hide full parties; the sort is newest or oldest first, lowest or highest Cata Level requirement, or easiest or hardest S+ PB requirement. The sort and filters are remembered (and ride in profiles). Create lists your party: the activity, party size, requirements (Cata Level, Secrets and an S+ PB for the floor, as m:ss, for dungeons) and note start from what you used last there. Join on someone's listing asks the leader through the relay; with Check Requirements on, your own stats are checked first, and a party whose requirements you are under - or whose requirements cannot be checked yet because your stats are still loading - is not asked. The relay refuses the same joins itself, so they never reach the leader. The leader sees each request with the joiner's stats against his requirements: one under any of them (or whose stats could not be read) shows as rejected with no Accept and the joiner is told; one that meets them has Accept and Decline, and with Auto Accept Requests on (the default) is invited straight away. Accept sends them a Hypixel party invite; with Auto Accept Invite on, the joiner's client accepts that leader's invite for them, once, within a minute. With Dual-List Hypixel Queues on, queuing a dungeon in Hypixel's Party Finder lists the same party here too. A listing goes when the party fills, when the party is left or disbanded, when the run starts, when you leave the server, or with Unlist (or /cpf unlist). Party commands are limited hard: invites at most one every 3 seconds and six a minute (the same name once per 30 seconds), and an accept only for a leader who accepted your own request.
- Dungeon Queue — Auto Requeue and Party Finder Overlay are two features with a switch each, and the menu card is ON while either is on and OFF only when both are (clicking it when both are off turns on Auto Requeue only). Auto Requeue (sends /instancerequeue at the end of a run after a delay, skipped if a party member leaves or is kicked) and Party Finder Overlay (green/red joinable highlight, member count, and tooltip stats: Catacombs level, secrets, PB for the floor, missing classes), with a live style preview, always shown at the bottom of its settings, that goes through the same code as the real tooltip; "Compact" is now called Party Finder style. Built-in styles: None, Style 1, Style 2, Compact, Minimal, Detailed, Stat Row and Class Tag. Custom is a style you write from placeholders ($Name, $NameColor, $Role, $RoleShort, $RoleSingle, $RoleColor, $RoleLevel, $Cata, $Secrets, $SecretsShort, $SecretAvg, $SecretShortAvg, $PB; & is a color code): placeholder buttons add themselves to the text, Load Built-in copies any built-in style in to start from, a line names any $Word that is not a placeholder, and Save Style / Saved Style / Delete keep any number of named custom styles (up to 32) that you step through with the Saved Style button The stats come first from killer560's relay, which keeps one cache shared by every user of the mod: the whole page goes in one request, anyone looked at recently shows at once, and the rest fill in as the relay fetches them (it polls every second or two, and a player older than 15 minutes in the cache shows straight away and updates a few seconds later if their PB changed). At the same time the mod fetches players itself from the Profile Viewer's source (the keyless SkyBlockPV backend, or your own Hypixel key if you set one there), three at a time, starting from the players the relay will reach last so the two meet in the middle instead of fetching the same people; it waits out the backend's rate limit (a 429 and its Retry-After) and retries a failed fetch after 15 s, 30 s, 1 and 2 minutes rather than showing ? for three minutes. The party you hover goes to the front of both (so does Auto Kick's joiner), then the rest of the open page, then anyone from earlier pages. Lookups keep running after you close the menu, so reopening it shows what arrived meanwhile; they stop when you change world (a dungeon starting, a lobby swap) or after 10 minutes. Answers are saved to disk for 3 days, so players you have seen before show at once and refresh in the background. Either way: Catacombs level from the selected profile (or, when none is selected or it has no dungeon data, the profile with the most Catacombs XP), secrets and average secrets over all profiles, and that profile's PB for the party's floor (Master Mode for a Master Mode party; Entrance too) - S+, S, or in Both mode S+ then S then the fastest clear at any score, shown gray and labeled "Any". Member lines are always in the chosen style, with ? for any stat not yet loaded (and a line under them saying it is still loading, or which source failed and why). Warns once if Devonian's own Party Finder Overview is on, since it replaces ours
- Inventory HUD — your main inventory drawn as a movable HUD panel (mini/normal, horizontal/vertical, background style, show always / hold key / toggle key). Until you move it in the HUD editor it sits centered above your hotbar, a little way up. The Opacity slider fades the panel background, outline and slots (0 hides the panel); the items are never dimmed - item textures cannot be tinted on 26.1.2, and the black overlay that used to fake it left a black box at any setting below 100, so it was removed on 2026-10-18.
- Skyblock Only — one toggle on the Home tab that pauses every feature outside Hypixel Skyblock and p3sim.net without changing any of your settings; rejoin Skyblock and everything is back on
- Sweat Mode — a toggle on the Home tab ("Click to enter sweat mode."): clicking it turns it on, opens a YouTube video in Google Chrome (the default browser if Chrome is not installed), and a second later it turns itself back off. Nothing is saved. The Home page also has it as a quick button ("Enters sweat mode.") next to Check Updates, under a YouTube button; the menu header's fourth round icon opens Killer560's YouTube channel (themes are picked in the Themes tab).
- Correction Alarm — **cheat build only.** A mod-wide rule since 2026-10-06: a server position correction never stops, pauses or switches off any automation (AP3, Auto Routes, the Interactive Map's runner, Auto Boulder, Auto Walk and the rest). Each one posts a chat line saying which feature, what it was doing and how far you were moved (at most one line every two seconds per feature, folding the rest into a count), plays an original siren-style alarm (at most once every three seconds), and the feature carries on from where the server put you, re-planning if it plans paths. Nothing ever moves you back. Only a move the server made on its own counts: the landing of a warp another of your features just sent (Auto Ice Fill's hops, a puzzle's reward chest warp) is that feature's own, so an Interactive Map trip that has already landed and is settling when one of them hops simply ends as arrived - no line, no alarm. Correction Alarm (on/off) and Alarm Volume (0-100%, default 100%) are on the Home tab.
- Freelook — the camera trick other mods call Freelook (Freelook OG, Free Look UTI, Decent Freelook; Shoulder Check and SimpleLook are the same idea). With it on, F5 (whatever key is bound to Toggle Perspective) goes straight to the backwards view, the camera in front of you looking back, and back to the view you had, instead of cycling all three. Mode Hold keeps the backwards view while F5 is held; Toggle switches on each press. Hold the Pan Key (a key or mouse button) to swing the camera around you with the mouse - from first person it shows the normal third-person view while held - and let go to have it return behind you. Camera only: your facing, movement and everything sent to the server stay exactly as they were. Off and the Pan Key unbound until you set them in Misc > Freelook
- Motion Blur — smooth frame-blending blur for high-FPS recordings (strength slider, optional GUI blur); turns itself off with a chat notice if the shader can't load
- Window Layout — pick how many game windows share a monitor, then click a cell in the picker to snap this window there at the right size (per-instance memory, optional restore on launch, taskbar-aware). Drawn in the mod menu's theme; its own Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or your Custom colors for this screen alone
- Real Time — a HUD clock from your computer's time (12h/24h, seconds, zone abbreviation) or any custom time zone
- Custom Scoreboard — SkyHanni-style replacement sidebar (ported from SkyHanni and SkyBlock Custom Scoreboard). It looks like a tidier vanilla sidebar: compact lines on a plain translucent box, with the title row a little darker, a thin orange border (Border Style Custom, on by default) and square corners. It sits where the vanilla scoreboard does - against the right edge of the screen, vertically centered - at every GUI scale and window size, and grows to the left when a line gets longer: its right wall, and the right end of its widest line, stay on exactly the same pixel whatever the lines, Text Align, Min Width, HUD scale or GUI scale (on the left edge, the same holds for its left wall and the left end of its text). Drag it anywhere in Edit HUD: dropped on the left half it anchors to the left edge instead, and it can never be drawn or dragged past any edge of the screen (a position saved by an older version that hung off screen is pulled back on). Reset Position (in the editor's Settings) puts it back on the right. Unknown lines still show (each is written to the log once; Unknown Line Warning, off by default, also notes it in chat once per kind of line); the dungeon board's "Solo" and "Keys:" lines are recognized however Hypixel colors them. Everything is edited in **Open Visual Editor** (the tab itself is just the on/off switch and that button): the board is drawn there filled with sample lines, and you drag the lines on the board itself to reorder them (an orange line shows where one will land). Drag a line onto the right-hand panel to take it off; the panel's **Not Active** list holds everything that is not on the board - click one to add it at the bottom, or drag it onto the board where it should go. Click a line on the board for its own settings (Mayor's perks and minister, Maxwell Tuning's amount, Party's leader and size, the Title and Footer text and alignment, and so on; Events and Chunked Stats show their own drag-to-order lists). The **Settings** page has what most people change - Background and its color, Text Shadow, Line Spacing, Numbers and Number Style, Hide With Tab List, Hide With Chat Open, Outside Skyblock, Clickable Lines and Reset Position - and one **Advanced** section, closed until you open it, with the rest: Hide Vanilla Scoreboard, Custom Lines, Text Align, the empty, irrelevant and separator line rules, Cache On Island Switch, Show Number Changes, Unknown Line Warning, 24h SkyBlock Time, Date Format, and the box (padding, rounded corners, border with gradient or a Chroma Border whose rainbow flows diagonally round it, border softness, background blur, min width and height, and a background image). Settings and boards saved before these changes load unchanged; a saved board still on the old orange look moves to the new one, keeping any look setting you had changed.
- Profile Viewer — `/pv [name]`: NEU-style Skyblock profile viewer (skills, slayers, dungeons with floor times, inventories/ender chest/backpacks/wardrobe/accessories, pets) with a 3D skin preview; a Weight page with Senither and Lily weight (skill/slayer/dungeon breakdown with overflow); source defaults to Auto, a recent-views sidebar (your own head pinned first, click a head or type a name to switch), Ender Chest and Backpacks tiled several pages at once, click-to-select Bestiary categories, and overflow levels on maxed skills - the number in brackets is the level your XP would reach with no cap at all, on the standard 60-level curve and past 60 the way SkyHanni counts it (level 61 costs 7.6M XP, each next level 600K more, that increase doubling every ten levels); Runecrafting and Social stop at their own tables. Every page tab fits on two rows. The name box under Switch is wide enough for a 16-character name and tab-completes: start typing and it lists matching names from the tab list, your recent views and anyone looked up this session; Tab cycles them (Shift+Tab back), a click opens one. Ender Chest pages and Backpacks are drawn as Storage Overlay panels (its theme, no backpack icon) and are clipped at the bottom of the page instead of running off it. Items show the Item Rarity background when that feature is on. Basic Info has a Networth line under Bank (the same estimate as the Networth page; click it to open that page), priced from the Bazaar and lowest BIN with no API key needed. The data's age is on the Refresh button's hover
- Profile Viewer — `/pv [name]`: NEU-style Skyblock profile viewer (skills, slayers, dungeons with floor times, inventories/ender chest/backpacks/wardrobe/accessories, pets) with a 3D skin preview; a Weight page with Senither and Lily weight (skill/slayer/dungeon breakdown with overflow); source defaults to Auto, a recent-views sidebar (your own head pinned first, click a head or type a name to switch), Ender Chest and Backpacks tiled several pages at once, click-to-select Bestiary categories, and overflow levels on maxed skills - the number in brackets is the level your XP would reach with no cap at all, on the standard 60-level curve and past 60 the way SkyHanni counts it (level 61 costs 7.6M XP, each next level 600K more, that increase doubling every ten levels); Runecrafting and Social stop at their own tables. Every page tab fits on two rows. The name box under Switch is wide enough for a 16-character name and tab-completes: start typing and it lists matching names from the tab list, your recent views and anyone looked up this session; Tab cycles them (Shift+Tab back), a click opens one. Ender Chest pages and Backpacks are drawn as Storage Overlay panels (its theme, no backpack icon) and are clipped at the bottom of the page instead of running off it. Items show the Item Rarity background when that feature is on. Basic Info has a Networth line under Bank (the same estimate as the Networth page; click it to open that page), priced from the Bazaar and lowest BIN with no API key needed. The data's age is on the Refresh button's hover. Drawn in the mod menu's theme; its own Theme setting starts on Theme Default (follows the menu) and can be set to Amber, Dark, Light, Ocean, Viridian or your Custom colors for this screen alone (the Storage Overlay panels keep the Storage Overlay's own theme)
- Themed Main Menu — the title screen redrawn in the mod's themes: an animated background, a framed panel holding the button column (Singleplayer, Multiplayer, Dungeon Sim, Swap Accounts, then Options and Quit under a thin rule; no Realms, language, accessibility, Friends or Mod Menu buttons), buttons with an accent ring, glow and side bar on hover, a "Playing as" chip with a green dot under the panel, and a thin accent line along the very bottom edge; the version line, the mod's name and version, and the copyright line are not shown. "Playing as" always shows the account's real name: Name Changer never recolors or renames anything on the main menu or the Swap Accounts screens. Every theme has two backgrounds, picked by Main Menu Scene (below): Fancy, its painted scene, and Simple, just floating particles in the theme's own colors and shading. The Simple scenes are one family: a dark backdrop in the theme's colors, a low glow along the bottom that breathes slowly, a fainter pool of light drifting beside it, and particles at three depths that thin out behind the buttons and stay clear of the title - Amber embers rising and flickering (the far ones tiny and dim, the near ones with a hot center and a soft halo) over a warm floor of light; Ocean bubbles rising and wobbling (each a faint body, a bright rim and a small highlight) with tiny motes drifting in the water, light from the surface above and a deep teal glow below; Dark a fine field of stars twinkling and drifting, dust rising slowly, a few soft near motes, two faint wandering nebula glows and the odd shooting star, nearly black and white; Viridian fireflies wandering and blinking in yellow-green with wide soft glows, and spores drifting down, over a mossy glow under a darker canopy. They are drawn in HD by the mod's own shaders at the screen's full resolution: every particle exactly antialiased and gliding between pixels, every glow and gradient dithered so nothing bands, and all of it moving smoothly at 120 FPS. The Fancy scenes: Amber's Aurora, killer560's own picture of an orange aurora over a blocky valley at sunset, with the baked-in title and buttons removed and every aurora band followed across the gaps they left, animated through a shader so all of it is smooth at full resolution - the curtains ripple gently, each ray flickers softly, light travels slowly along the bands, the stars twinkle, the low sun's glow breathes, glints drift on the river, the lava on the right mountain pulses and a few embers drift up at the sides; the view itself never moves; the older Amber scenes - the drifting embers, NEW, a dungeon colonnade at dusk, and the first calm embers - stay in the code behind `MainMenuScenes.AMBER_SCENE`), Viridian the theme designer's own picture of the slime boss, animated (drawn from a 2560x1440 copy of it, smoothly filtered, with its glows and drifting fog smooth and dithered, and the waterfall streaks and lake shimmer gliding between pixels), or a slime boss (the acid cave below, with a huge glowing Minecraft slime crowned with light rising out of the lake under the waterfall and a big close-up silhouette of the theme designer's character facing it, sword in hand;
